<section class="features container">
<h1 class="heading features__heading">What can I do with Mailtrap?</h1>
<div class="feature">
<img alt="feature no spam" class="feature__image" draggable="false" loading="lazy" src="/assets/landing/home/feature-no-spam-6c131e5f83cf4b05c7fab28203d31ae350f2aebba19dd8e931fced1ca3b9a05b.svg"/>
<div class="feature__description feature__description--bottom-aligned">
<h1 class="heading feature__title"><span class="heading-heighlight heading-heighlight--secondary">Never spam</span> real customers by mistake</h1>
<p class="paragraph">Mailtrap is a dedicated playground for your email tests. Use it before your messages are perfect enough for reaching real inboxes</p>
</div>
</div>
<div class="feature">
<img alt="feature analysis" class="feature__image" draggable="false" loading="lazy" src="/assets/landing/home/feature-analysis-baacb64589f62a5869ebcfd59c9747aa64d0ff9993ffe6669ee7bb7b9d5fe095.svg"/>
<div class="feature__description">
<h1 class="heading feature__title"><span class="heading-heighlight">Analyze emails</span> for spam &amp; blacklists</h1>
<p class="paragraph">Stay on the safe side with your emails. Mailtrap checks your domain and gives recommendations how to avoid spam filters</p>
</div>
</div>
<div class="feature">
<img alt="feature testing" class="feature__image" draggable="false" loading="lazy" src="/assets/landing/home/feature-testing-7cfdbb81e9d658181536b36a7196bae907a3c2a294f73f81b1c2d202e869f51d.svg"/>
<div class="feature__description">
<h1 class="heading feature__title"><span class="heading-heighlight heading-heighlight--postsecondary">Test HTML</span> for support by basic email clients</h1>
<p class="paragraph">Inspect and debug each part of your message, and check if it works fine for the most popular email clients.</p>
</div>
</div>
<div class="feature">
<img alt="feature sharing" class="feature__image" draggable="false" loading="lazy" src="/assets/landing/home/feature-sharing-0d9aab661fb2510aebdfea0d9b7bd1b395c1e9295c3b794491e8e7c283fde974.svg"/>
<div class="feature__description">
<h1 class="heading feature__title"><span class="heading-heighlight heading-heighlight--secondary">Share inboxes</span> with your team members</h1>
<p class="paragraph">Discuss your ideas with colleagues or demonstrate your progress to the customer: invite them to your inbox!</p>
</div>
</div>
<div class="feature">
<img alt="feature inboxes" class="feature__image" draggable="false" loading="lazy" src="/assets/landing/home/feature-inboxes-121af0babab76f765fa706f22efa5cc6faf863103e5dc3214e13930c3725dd59.svg"/>
<div class="feature__description">
<h1 class="heading feature__title"><span class="heading-heighlight">Organize</span> test emails into special inboxes</h1>
<p class="paragraph">Separate your projects by creating a new inbox for each of them, just in two clicks. No more chaos!</p>
</div>
</div>
<div class="feature">
<img alt="feature security" class="feature__image" draggable="false" loading="lazy" src="/assets/landing/home/feature-security-aeb3e85372ec753013f02078fd19c721a575ef75027b946dab1b55ac2497dd4a.svg"/>
<div class="feature__description">
<h1 class="heading feature__title"><span class="heading-heighlight heading-heighlight--postsecondary">Secure by default</span> (HTTPS &amp; TLS)</h1>
<p class="paragraph">Your connection with the Mailtrap server is secure and encrypted. Your data always stays safe and private</p>
</div>
</div>
</section>

<div class="footer-copyright__text">
<p>What is Mailtrap?</p><p>
</p><p>Mailtrap is a test mail server solution that allows testing email notifications without sending them to the real users of your application. Not only does Mailtrap work as a powerful email test tool, it also lets you view your dummy emails online, forward them to your regular mailbox, share with the team and more! Mailtrap is a mail server test tool built by Railsware Products, Inc., a premium software development consulting company.</p>
